        Idea:
# Wire timeline drop to use the existing local-file-handle flow

Read CLAUDE.md at the repo root first — it has cross-cutting rules and points to relevant sub-docs.

## Problem

When a user drops a large file onto the **timeline**, it always uploads to Supabase Storage. It should instead use the existing local-file-handle flow (`storage_mode='local'` + `local_handle_id`, served via `useLocalMediaUrl()` with `LocalMediaPermissionOverlay` as the recovery UX) — the same way the gallery's `useDropToGeneration` already works for large files.

## What already exists (do NOT rebuild)

- **Local-handle write path**: `src/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile.ts` — `createGenerationForLocalFile()` writes `storage_mode='local'` + `local_handle_id` and persists the handle via `saveHandle()` from `src/shared/lib/media/localHandleStore.ts`.
- **Threshold + browser-capability gate**: `src/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration.ts` checks file size against `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` from `src/shared/lib/media/dropToGenerationConfig.ts` AND that `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()` is available. Use the **same** gate.
- **Resolver + permission UX**: `useLocalMediaUrl()` in `src/shared/media/localMediaResolver.ts` and `LocalMediaPermissionOverlay.tsx` already handle rendering + recovery — no changes needed there.

## What's broken

The timeline drop pipeline is a separate, older path that never got updated:

- Drop handler chain ends at `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` → calls `processDroppedImages` in `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts`.
- `processDroppedImages` only receives `File[]`. It calls `processSingleDroppedImage`, which **unconditionally** calls `createGenerationForUploadedImage`. No size check, no `getAsFileSystemHandle()` probe, no local branch.

There is also an intermediate chain of timeline drop hooks (in the travel-between-images tool) that strips/discards `DataTransferItem` info before it reaches `useDropActions`. Those hooks must be updated to capture and forward handles.

## The fix

1. **Capture the `FileSystemFileHandle` synchronously** at the timeline drop event boundary. The `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()` call MUST happen synchronously inside the drop handler (or its returned promises captured before any await), because `DataTransferItem` is invalidated after the event resolves. Look at how `useDropToGeneration` does this and replicate the pattern.

2. **Thread an optional `FileSystemFileHandle | null` array through** the timeline drop hooks → `useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop` → `ExternalImageDropVariables` → `processDroppedImages` → `processSingleDroppedImage`. The new array is parallel to `imageFiles`. Make it optional so non-timeline callers aren't broken.

3. **Branch in `processSingleDroppedImage`** using the same gate as `useDropToGeneration` — file size ≥ `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND a handle is present. When the gate passes, call `createGenerationForLocalFile`. Otherwise keep current behavior (`createGenerationForUploadedImage`).

4. **Crop interaction (important)**: `cropFilesIfNeeded` may return a *new* `File` for a given input. If it did, the captured handle no longer corresponds to the bytes you're storing — fall back to upload in that case. Use reference equality (`processedFile === originalFile`) to detect this.

5. **Don't break callers.** The new param is optional. Other callers of `processDroppedImages` (batch drop, app-level drop, shot-creation flows) keep current behavior unchanged because they don't supply handles.

## Constraints

- Don't refactor unrelated code. Don't add new abstractions. Don't add comments explaining the change.
- Reuse existing helpers from `useDropToGeneration` if possible (e.g. the `supportsLocalFileHandles()` probe pattern). Keep the gate condition identical to what the gallery uses so behavior stays consistent.
- The `local_handle_id` is a UUID generated client-side and is the link between the DB row and the IndexedDB-persisted handle. Mirror exactly how the gallery flow generates and stores it.

## Tests

- Mirror the patterns in `src/features/gallery/hooks/useDropToGeneration.test.tsx` and the existing `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts` (if present).
- Add at least two cases on the `externalImageDrop` side: (a) large file + handle → local-handle path is taken, (b) small file or no handle → upload path is taken.

## Verification

- Run `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json`. Note: the codebase has pre-existing type errors. Your changes must not introduce *new* errors beyond the pre-existing pattern that already affects `useDropToGeneration.ts` (`FileSystemFileHandle` missing `queryPermission`/`requestPermission` lib types).
- Run vitest on the affected directories: `src/shared/hooks/shots`, `src/features/gallery/hooks`, and any timeline-drop hook directory you modify.

## Deliverables

- All changed files with the wiring in place.
- New/updated tests covering both branches.
- A short note in your final summary listing every caller of `processDroppedImages` and confirming each one still works.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Wire timeline drop to use existing local-file-handle flow

## Overview
The timeline image-drop pipeline unconditionally uploads to Supabase Storage via `createGenerationForUploadedImage`. The gallery (`useDropToGeneration`) already has a size-gated local-handle branch using `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()`. We thread a `FileSystemFileHandle[]` from the drag event boundary through the timeline drop chain into `processDroppedImages`/`processSingleDroppedImage`, branch at the same gate (`size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` + handle present), and call `createGenerationForLocalFile` instead of `createGenerationForUploadedImage`.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Capture `FileSystemFileHandle[]` at the drop event boundary (`useUnifiedDrop.ts`)
**Scope:** Small

The `useUnifiedDrop.handleDrop` at line 103 is where `React.DragEvent` hits. Currently line 134 extracts `e.dataTransfer.files` but discards `e.dataTransfer.items`. We must call `getAsFileSystemHandle()` synchronously here (capturing the promise before the event resolves).

1. **Before** extracting files, iterate `e.dataTransfer.items`, filter for `kind === 'file'`, and call `getAsFileSystemHandle()` on each, collecting promises into an array.
2. **After** `filterValidTimelineImageFiles` (line 140), if the count matches, resolve the handle promises and pass them alongside `validFiles`.
3. The `onFileDrop` signature currently takes `(files: File[], targetFrame?: number)`. We need a new optional third parameter — but we cannot change the prop interface without cascading changes through `useUnifiedDropProps` → `ShotImagesEditor` → etc. Instead, thread handles through a **new optional parameter on `onFileDrop`**. The `useUnifiedDropProps.onFileDrop` gains `handles?: (FileSystemFileHandle | null)[]`.

Files touched:
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useUnifiedDrop.ts:17-18` (update `UseUnifiedDropProps.onFileDrop` signature)
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useUnifiedDrop.ts:103-150` (capture handles, pass them)

### Step 2: Thread handles through timeline drop hooks → `useDropActions`
**Scope:** Small

`useUnifiedDrop` passes `onFileDrop` which is wired through `ShotImagesEditorContent` → `TimelineSection` → `imageHandlers.onFileDrop` (from `useShotSettingsValue`) → `generationActions.handleTimelineImageDrop`. Each layer must forward the new `handles` parameter.

1. `useShotSettingsValue.ts:210` — the `onFileDrop` callback already calls `generationActions.handleTimelineImageDrop(files, targetFrame)`. Add the handles parameter.
2. `useDropActions.ts:187` — `handleTimelineImageDrop` currently takes `(files: File[], targetFrame?: number)`. Add optional `handles?: (FileSystemFileHandle | null)[]`.
3. Inside `handleTimelineImageDrop`, the handles need to be tracked through the crop step. `cropImagesToShotAspectRatio` (line 252) may return new `File` objects. We need the **original files** alongside handles to do reference-equality check later. Store the original files array before cropping, then pass handles + original files into the `processDroppedImages` call.

Files touched:
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/editor-state/useShotSettingsValue.ts:33,210-211`
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useDropActions.ts:187,252-266`
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useGenerationActions.ts:61,79,88` (interface type)
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/ShotSettingsContext.types.ts` (if `onFileDrop` type lives there)

### Step 3: Add handles to `ExternalImageDropVariables` and `processDroppedImages`
**Scope:** Small

1. Add optional `handles?: (FileSystemFileHandle | null)[]` to `ExternalImageDropVariables` (`externalImageDrop.ts:9-17`).
2. Pass it through `processDroppedImages` into `processSingleDroppedImage`.
3. Also store the `originalFiles` (pre-crop) so we can detect crop at the right level.

Files touched:
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:9-17` (`ExternalImageDropVariables`)
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:248-310` (`processSingleDroppedImage` signature and body)
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:311-357` (`processDroppedImages` signature and body)

### Step 4: Branch in `processSingleDroppedImage`
**Scope:** Medium

Inside `processSingleDroppedImage`, before the unconditional `createGeneration` call (line 271-277):

1. Determine if the file was cropped: compare `imageFile` (post-crop from `processedFiles`) with the original file at the same `fileIndex`. If different references → upload path.
2. If NOT cropped AND `imageFile.size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` AND `handles?.[fileIndex]` is truthy → call `createGenerationForLocalFile({ file: imageFile, projectId, handle: handles[fileIndex], mediaType: 'image' })`.
3. Otherwise, keep current `createGeneration` call (upload path).

Import `createGenerationForLocalFile` and `IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES` at the top of `externalImageDrop.ts`.

**Crop interaction detail**: `cropFilesIfNeeded` (line 325 in `processDroppedImages`) returns potentially modified files. Pass the original `imageFiles` array (the input to `cropFilesIfNeeded`) down to `processSingleDroppedImage` as `originalFiles`. In `processSingleDroppedImage`, compare `processedFiles[fileIndex] === originalFiles[fileIndex]`. If they differ, the handle no longer matches the bytes → fall back to upload.

Files touched:
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:1-8` (new imports)
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:248-310` (branch logic)
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:311-357` (pass original files through)

### Step 5: Handle `ProcessDroppedImagesInput.createGeneration` type
**Scope:** Small

The `createGeneration` parameter in `ProcessDroppedImagesInput` is typed as `typeof createGenerationForUploadedImage`. With the branch, we sometimes use `createGenerationForLocalFile` which has a different signature. The local-handle path bypasses the injected `createGeneration` callback entirely (it directly calls `createGenerationForLocalFile`). This is fine — no type change needed.

### Step 6: Add/update tests
**Scope:** Medium

1. **`externalImageDrop.test.ts`**: Add two cases:
   - (a) Large file (≥ 25MB) + valid handle → `createGenerationForLocalFile` is called. Mock `createGenerationForLocalFile` via `vi.mock('@/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile', ...)` and assert it was called.
   - (b) Small file (< 25MB) or no handle → upload path taken (existing behavior). Assert `createGenerationForUploadedImage` was called.
   - (c) Cropped file (where result !== original) even with handle → falls back to upload.

2. **`useDropActions.test.ts` near line 336+**: Update existing `handleTimelineImageDrop` tests to verify the handles parameter flows through. Add a test with handles passed and verify `processDroppedImages` receives them.

3. **`useUnifiedDrop.test.ts`** (if present): Verify that `DataTransferItem.getAsFileSystemHandle()` is called and result passed through.

Files touched:
- `src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts`
- `src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/__tests__/useDropActions.test.ts`

### Step 7: Verification
**Scope:** Small

1. Run `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` and confirm no *new* errors (pre-existing `FileSystemFileHandle` lib type gaps from `useDropToGeneration.ts` are expected and acceptable).
2. Run `npx vitest run src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.test.ts`
3. Run `npx vitest run src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/__tests__/useDropActions.test.ts`
4. Run `npx vitest run src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/` (if tests exist)

## Execution Order
1. Steps 1-3 (threading): Update all signatures and pass-through points first, test-compile.
2. Step 4 (branch logic): Implement the core gate.
3. Step 6 (tests): Verify both branches.
4. Step 7 (verification): Full type-check + test run.

## Caller Audit
Callers of `processDroppedImages` (one callsite in non-test code):
1. `useShotCreation.ts:78` — `useHandleExternalImageDrop.mutateAsync` wraps `processDroppedImages`. Called from:
   - `useModalImageHandlers.ts:81` (modal image drop → `onFileDrop`)
   - `useDropActions.ts:266` (timeline drop → `handleTimelineImageDrop`)
   - `useDropActions.ts:486` (batch mode drop → `handleBatchImageDrop`)
   - `ShotListView.tsx` (shot-level external drop)
   - `App.tsx:38` (app-level global drop handler)

All callers pass `ExternalImageDropVariables` via mutation. With the `handles` field being optional (`?`), none of these break. Only timeline-drop callers that now supply handles will trigger the local-handle branch. Others (batch, app-level, shot-list view) continue using upload for all files unchanged.

    {
      "id": "DEBT-131",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",

      "id": "DEBT-132",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 11.2 specifies the reverse switch is 'single commit, no `start`/`children`/`offset` reads anywhere' but doesn't state the mutation type explicitly. since the step describes metadeletes + cliporderoverride + pinnedshotgroupsoverride in a single commit, the mutation must be `type: 'rows'` (the only variant carrying all three). self-consistent with step 11.1's restriction on `type: 'pinnedshotgroups'`, but making the mutation type explicit would reduce executor ambiguity.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "roll-back-the-first-class-20260408-1337"

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the current imperative selection adapters in `useclipdrag.ts`, `usetimelinepersistence.ts`, `usepollsync.ts`, and `videoeditorprovider.tsx`. the mutable refs (`selectedclipidsref`, `additiveselectionref`, `selectedclipidref`, `selectedtrackidref`) are runtime adapters for drag/save/poll callers rather than durable domain state, so the plan's wording about putting 'refs/helpers' into the `timeline` slice is a design error unless it explicitly keeps store state separate from ref adapters rebuilt around that state.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "refactor-pinned-shot-groups-20260408-0508"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual callers of the timeline-selection refs with `rg 'selectedclipidsref|additiveselectionref|selectedclipidref|selectedtrackidref' src/tools/video-editor`. save/poll paths consume `selectedclipidref` and `selectedtrackidref`, but `useclipdrag.ts`, `timelineeditor.tsx`, `videoeditorprovider.tsx`, and `usetimelinestate.contexts.ts` also consume `selectedclipidsref` and `additiveselectionref`, so the plan does not yet handle all caller shapes unless it adds a store-backed adapter for the multi-select refs as well.",

      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `setselectedclipid(` callers in `useassetmanagement.ts`, `usecliptextoverlay.ts`, `propertiespanel.tsx`, and `previewpanel.tsx`. those callers pass concrete clip ids and `null` through the existing dispatch-like api, so any store action that replaces `setselectedclipid` must preserve the same semantics for clearing and reselection rather than narrowing the contract to a simpler single-purpose setter.",

      "id": "DEBT-176",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelinecanvas.tsx` as a caller of `usetimelineeditordata()`. it only reads `dataref`, but it passes that mutable ref straight into `useclipresizegesture`, which relies on latest timeline data during pointer sequences; the plan does not yet say how this exact caller will receive a stable mutable adapter once the context is removed.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-177",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked `src/tools/video-editor/components/videoeditorshell.tsx`, where `handlekeyboarddelete` combines `editordata.dataref.current` with `editordata.selectedclipids` to build a mutation before falling back to `handledeleteclips`. that caller needs synchronized latest-ref and latest-selection semantics, so splitting those reads across unrelated store selectors without an adapter can change behavior even if each field is individually correct.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "milestone-m1c-from-docs-state-20260416-2053"
      ]

      "id": "DEBT-188",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual attachment merge callers: `agentchat.tsx` currently passes timeline clips from `useselectedmediaclips()` and gallery clips from `usegalleryselection().selectedgalleryclips` into the merge. because both caller paths supply `clipid`, a `clipid || url` key handles placeholder timeline clips but no longer matches the real gallery-vs-timeline same-url caller case that the old url-keyed merge handled.",

        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",

1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
3	import { toast } from '@/shared/components/ui/runtime/sonner';
4	import { useProjectSelectionContext } from '@/shared/contexts/ProjectContext';
5	import { normalizeAndPresentError } from '@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError';
6	import {
7	  createGenerationForLocalFile,
8	  createGenerationForUploadedImage,
9	  createGenerationForUploadedVideo,
10	} from '@/shared/lib/media/createGenerationFromFile';
11	import {
12	  IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES,
13	  VIDEO_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES,
14	} from '@/shared/lib/media/dropToGenerationConfig';
15	import { unifiedGenerationQueryKeys } from '@/shared/lib/queryKeys/unified';
16	
17	function isImageFile(file: File): boolean {
18	  return file.type.startsWith('image/');
19	}
20	
21	function isVideoFile(file: File): boolean {
22	  return file.type.startsWith('video/');
23	}
24	
25	function formatLimitMb(bytes: number): number {
26	  return Math.round(bytes / (1024 * 1024));
27	}
28	
29	type FileDropHandleItem = DataTransferItem & {
30	  getAsFileSystemHandle?: () => Promise<FileSystemHandle | null>;
31	};
32	
33	type FileSystemHandleLike = FileSystemHandle & {
34	  getFile?: () => Promise<File>;
35	};
36	
37	interface DropToGenerationOptions {
38	  items?: Iterable<DataTransferItem>;
39	}
40	
41	function supportsLocalFileHandles(): boolean {
42	  return typeof DataTransferItem !== 'undefined'
43	    && typeof (DataTransferItem.prototype as FileDropHandleItem).getAsFileSystemHandle === 'function';
44	}
45	
46	function isReadableFileHandle(handle: FileSystemHandleLike | null): handle is FileSystemFileHandle {
47	  return !!handle && handle.kind === 'file' && typeof handle.getFile === 'function';
48	}
49	
50	export function useDropToGeneration(): (files: File[], options?: DropToGenerationOptions) => Promise<void> {
51	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
52	  const { selectedProjectId } = useProjectSelectionContext();
53	
54	  return useCallback(async (files: File[], options?: DropToGenerationOptions) => {
55	    if (!selectedProjectId) {
56	      toast.error('Please select a project first');
57	      return;
58	    }
59	
60	    const dropItems = Array.from(options?.items ?? []).filter((item): item is FileDropHandleItem => item.kind === 'file');
61	    const canUseLocalHandles = supportsLocalFileHandles();
62	    let insertedCount = 0;
63	
64	    for (const [index, file] of files.entries()) {
65	      try {
66	        if (isImageFile(file)) {
67	          if (file.size >= IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES) {
68	            const item = dropItems[index];
69	            if (!canUseLocalHandles || !item?.getAsFileSystemHandle) {
70	              toast.info(
71	                `${file.name} is ${formatLimitMb(IMAGE_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES)}MB+ and will use the browser fallback flow in a later batch.`,
72	              );
73	              continue;
74	            }
75	
76	            const handle = await item.getAsFileSystemHandle();
77	            if (!isReadableFileHandle(handle)) {
78	              toast.info(
79	                `${file.name} could not keep a local file handle. The browser fallback flow will arrive in a later batch.`,
80	              );
81	              continue;
82	            }
83	
84	            await createGenerationForLocalFile({
85	              file,
86	              projectId: selectedProjectId,
87	              handle,
88	              mediaType: 'image',
89	            });
90	            insertedCount += 1;
91	            continue;
92	          }
93	
94	          await createGenerationForUploadedImage({
95	            imageFile: file,
96	            projectId: selectedProjectId,
97	          });
98	          insertedCount += 1;
99	          continue;
100	        }
101	
102	        if (isVideoFile(file)) {
103	          if (file.size >= VIDEO_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES) {
104	            const item = dropItems[index];
105	            if (!canUseLocalHandles || !item?.getAsFileSystemHandle) {
106	              toast.info(
107	                `${file.name} is ${formatLimitMb(VIDEO_INLINE_UPLOAD_LIMIT_BYTES)}MB+ and will use the browser fallback flow in a later batch.`,
108	              );
109	              continue;
110	            }
111	
112	            const handle = await item.getAsFileSystemHandle();
113	            if (!isReadableFileHandle(handle)) {
114	              toast.info(
115	                `${file.name} could not keep a local file handle. The browser fallback flow will arrive in a later batch.`,
116	              );
117	              continue;
118	            }
119	
120	            await createGenerationForLocalFile({
121	              file,
122	              projectId: selectedProjectId,
123	              handle,
124	              mediaType: 'video',
125	            });
126	            insertedCount += 1;
127	            continue;
128	          }
129	
130	          await createGenerationForUploadedVideo({
131	            videoFile: file,
132	            projectId: selectedProjectId,
133	          });
134	          insertedCount += 1;
135	          continue;
136	        }
137	
138	        toast.error(`Unsupported file type: ${file.name}`);
139	      } catch (error) {
140	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
141	          context: 'useDropToGeneration',
142	          toastTitle: `Failed to add ${file.name}`,
143	        });
144	      }
145	    }
146	
147	    if (insertedCount > 0) {
148	      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({
149	        [REDACTED](selectedProjectId),
150	      });
151	    }
152	  }, [queryClient, selectedProjectId]);
153	}
154

63	const buildProgressHandler = (

76	async function getCropConfig(projectId: string, shotId: string | null): Promise<CropConfig> {
77	  const config: CropConfig = {
78	    shouldCrop: true,
79	    targetAspectRatio: null,
80	    aspectRatioSource: 'none',
81	  };
82	
83	  try {
84	    const { data: projectData } = await supabase().from('projects')
85	      .select('aspect_ratio, settings')
86	      .eq('id', projectId)
87	      .single();
88	
89	    let shotAspectRatio: string | null = null;
90	    if (shotId) {
91	      const { data: shotData } = await supabase().from('shots')
92	        .select('aspect_ratio')
93	        .eq('id', shotId)
94	        .single();
95	      shotAspectRatio = shotData?.aspect_ratio || null;
96	    }
97	
98	    const uploadSettings = (projectData?.settings as Record<string, unknown> | null)?.upload as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
99	    const cropToProjectSize = uploadSettings?.cropToProjectSize;
100	    config.shouldCrop = typeof cropToProjectSize === 'boolean' ? cropToProjectSize : true;
101	
102	    const effectiveRatioStr = shotAspectRatio || projectData?.aspect_ratio || null;
103	    if (effectiveRatioStr) {
104	      config.targetAspectRatio = parseRatio(effectiveRatioStr);
105	      config.aspectRatioSource = shotAspectRatio ? 'shot' : 'project';
106	    }
107	  } catch (error) {
108	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation:aspectRatio', showToast: false });
109	  }
110	
111	  return config;
112	}
113	
114	async function cropFilesIfNeeded(imageFiles: File[], config: CropConfig): Promise<File[]> {
115	  const { shouldCrop, targetAspectRatio } = config;
116	  if (!shouldCrop || !targetAspectRatio || Number.isNaN(targetAspectRatio)) {
117	    return imageFiles;
118	  }
119	
120	  try {
121	    const cropPromises = imageFiles.map(async (file) => {
122	      try {
123	        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) {
124	          return file;
125	        }
126	
127	        const result = await cropImageToProjectAspectRatio(file, targetAspectRatio);
128	        return result?.croppedFile || file;
129	      } catch (error) {
130	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: `useShotCreation:crop:${file.name}`, showToast: false });
131	        return file;
132	      }
133	    });
134	
135	    return await Promise.all(cropPromises);
136	  } catch (error) {
137	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation:batchCrop', showToast: false });
138	    return imageFiles;
139	  }
140	}
141	
142	async function ensureTargetShotId(
143	  targetShotId: string | null,
144	  currentShotCount: number,
145	  projectId: string,
146	  createShot: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['createShot'],
147	): Promise<string | null> {
148	  if (targetShotId) {
149	    return targetShotId;
150	  }
151	
152	  const newShotName = `Shot ${currentShotCount + 1}`;
153	  const result = await createShot({
154	    name: newShotName,
155	    projectId,
156	    shouldSelectAfterCreation: true,
157	  });
158	
159	  if (!result?.shot?.id) {
160	    toast.error('Failed to create new shot.');
161	    return null;
162	  }
163	
164	  return result.shot.id;
165	}

167	async function attachGenerationToShot(input: {
168	  shotId: string;
169	  generation: Database['public']['Tables']['generations']['Row'];
170	  projectId: string;
171	  thumbnailUrl?: string | null;
172	  fileIndex: number;
173	  variables: ExternalImageDropVariables;
174	  addImageToShot: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShot'];
175	  addImageToShotWithoutPosition: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShotWithoutPosition'];
176	}): Promise<{ shot_generation_id: string; timeline_frame: number | null }> {
177	  const {
178	    shotId,
179	    generation,
180	    projectId,
181	    thumbnailUrl,
182	    fileIndex,
183	    variables,
184	    addImageToShot,
185	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
186	  } = input;
187	
188	  const { positions, skipAutoPosition } = variables;
189	  const explicitPosition = positions && positions.length > fileIndex ? positions[fileIndex] : undefined;
190	  const baseInput = {
191	    shot_id: shotId,
192	    generation_id: generation.id as string,
193	    project_id: projectId,
194	    imageUrl: generation.location || undefined,
195	    thumbUrl: thumbnailUrl || generation.location || undefined,
196	  };
197	
198	  if (explicitPosition !== undefined) {
199	    const result = await addImageToShot({
200	      ...baseInput,
201	      timelineFrame: explicitPosition,
202	    });
203	    const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
204	      ? (result as { id: string }).id
205	      : null;
206	    if (!shotGenerationId) {
207	      throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation with explicit position.');
208	    }
209	    return {
210	      shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
211	      timeline_frame: typeof (result as { timeline_frame?: unknown })?.timeline_frame === 'number'
212	        ? (result as { timeline_frame: number }).timeline_frame
213	        : explicitPosition,
214	    };
215	  }
216	
217	  if (skipAutoPosition) {
218	    const result = await addImageToShotWithoutPosition(baseInput);
219	    const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
220	      ? (result as { id: string }).id
221	      : null;
222	    if (!shotGenerationId) {
223	      throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation without position.');
224	    }
225	    return {
226	      shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
227	      timeline_frame: null,
228	    };
229	  }
230	
231	  const result = await addImageToShot({
232	    ...baseInput,
233	  });
234	  const shotGenerationId = typeof (result as { id?: unknown })?.id === 'string'
235	    ? (result as { id: string }).id
236	    : null;
237	  if (!shotGenerationId) {
238	    throw new Error('Failed to attach uploaded generation to shot.');
239	  }
240	  return {
241	    shot_generation_id: shotGenerationId,
242	    timeline_frame: typeof (result as { timeline_frame?: unknown })?.timeline_frame === 'number'
243	      ? (result as { timeline_frame: number }).timeline_frame
244	      : null,
245	  };
246	}
247	
248	async function processSingleDroppedImage(input: {
249	  imageFile: File;
250	  fileIndex: number;
251	  totalFiles: number;
252	  shotId: string;
253	  projectId: string;
254	  variables: ExternalImageDropVariables;
255	  addImageToShot: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShot'];
256	  addImageToShotWithoutPosition: ProcessDroppedImagesInput['addImageToShotWithoutPosition'];
257	  createGeneration: typeof createGenerationForUploadedImage;
258	}): Promise<UploadedGenerationMetadata | null> {
259	  const {
260	    imageFile,
261	    fileIndex,
262	    totalFiles,
263	    shotId,
264	    projectId,
265	    variables,
266	    addImageToShot,
267	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
268	    createGeneration,
269	  } = input;
270	
271	  let generation: Database['public']['Tables']['generations']['Row'];
272	  try {
273	    generation = await createGeneration({
274	      imageFile,
275	      projectId,
276	      onProgress: buildProgressHandler(variables.onProgress, fileIndex, totalFiles),
277	    });
278	  } catch (error) {
279	    toast.error(`Failed to create generation data for ${imageFile.name}: ${(error as Error).message}`);
280	    return null;
281	  }
282	
283	  if (!generation?.id) {
284	    toast.error(`Failed to create generation record for ${imageFile.name}.`);
285	    return null;
286	  }
287	
288	  const attachment = await attachGenerationToShot({
289	    shotId,
290	    generation,
291	    projectId,
292	    thumbnailUrl: generation.thumbnail_url ?? generation.location,
293	    fileIndex,
294	    variables,
295	    addImageToShot,
296	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
297	  });
298	
299	  return {
300	    generationId: generation.id,
301	    location: generation.location,
302	    thumbnail_url: generation.thumbnail_url ?? generation.location,
303	    type: generation.type,
304	    created_at: generation.created_at,
305	    params: generation.params,
306	    primary_variant_id: generation.primary_variant_id,
307	    shot_generation_id: attachment.shot_generation_id,
308	    timeline_frame: attachment.timeline_frame,
309	  };
310	}
311	export async function processDroppedImages(
312	  input: ProcessDroppedImagesInput,
313	): Promise<{ shotId: string; generationIds: string[]; generationMetadata: UploadedGenerationMetadata[] } | null> {
314	  const {
315	    variables,
316	    projectId,
317	    createShot,
318	    addImageToShot,
319	    addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
320	    createGeneration = createGenerationForUploadedImage,
321	  } = input;
322	  const { imageFiles, targetShotId, currentShotCount } = variables;
323	
324	  const cropConfig = await getCropConfig(projectId, targetShotId);
325	  const processedFiles = await cropFilesIfNeeded(imageFiles, cropConfig);
326	  const shotId = await ensureTargetShotId(targetShotId, currentShotCount, projectId, createShot);
327	  if (!shotId) {
328	    return null;
329	  }
330	
331	  const generationIds: string[] = [];
332	  const generationMetadata: UploadedGenerationMetadata[] = [];
333	  for (let fileIndex = 0; fileIndex < processedFiles.length; fileIndex++) {
334	    const imageFile = processedFiles[fileIndex];
335	    try {
336	      const metadata = await processSingleDroppedImage({
337	        imageFile,
338	        fileIndex,
339	        totalFiles: processedFiles.length,
340	        shotId,
341	        projectId,
342	        variables,
343	        addImageToShot,
344	        addImageToShotWithoutPosition,
345	        createGeneration,
346	      });
347	      if (metadata) {
348	        generationIds.push(metadata.generationId);
349	        generationMetadata.push(metadata);
350	      }
351	    } catch (error) {
352	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation', toastTitle: `Failed to process file ${imageFile.name}` });
353	    }
354	  }
355	
356	  return generationIds.length > 0 ? { shotId, generationIds, generationMetadata } : null;
357	}

1	import { useState, useCallback } from "react";
2	import { normalizeAndPresentError } from "@/shared/lib/errorHandling/runtimeError";
3	import { pixelToFrame } from "../../utils/timeline-utils";
4	import { TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET } from "../../constants";
5	import {
6	  getDragType as sharedGetDragType,
7	  getGenerationDropData,
8	  wasDropHandledByVariant,
9	  type DragType,
10	  type GenerationDropData
11	} from "@/shared/lib/dnd/dragDrop";
12	import { filterValidTimelineImageFiles } from "./imageDropValidation";
13	
14	// Re-export for backward compatibility
15	export type { DragType, GenerationDropData };
16	
17	interface UseUnifiedDropProps {
18	  onFileDrop?: (files: File[], targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
19	  onGenerationDrop?: (generationId: string, imageUrl: string, thumbUrl: string | undefined, targetFrame?: number) => Promise<void>;
20	  fullMin: number;
21	  fullRange: number;
22	}
23	
24	/**
25	 * Unified drop hook that handles both file drops (from file system) and generation drops (from GenerationsPane)
26	 * Reuses the same coordinate system and visual feedback for consistency
27	 */
28	export const useUnifiedDrop = ({
29	  onFileDrop,
30	  onGenerationDrop,
31	  fullMin,
32	  fullRange
33	}: UseUnifiedDropProps) => {
34	  const [isFileOver, setIsFileOver] = useState(false);
35	  const [isGenerationOver, setIsGenerationOver] = useState(false);
36	  const [dropTargetFrame, setDropTargetFrame] = useState<number | null>(null);
37	
38	  /**
39	   * Detect the type of item being dragged (wrapper around shared utility for logging)
40	   */
41	  const getDragType = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>): DragType => {
42	    const dragType = sharedGetDragType(e);
43	    
44	    return dragType;
45	  }, []);
46	
47	  const handleDragEnter = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
48	    e.preventDefault();
49	    e.stopPropagation();
50	    
51	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
52	    
53	    if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
54	      setIsFileOver(true);
55	    } else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
56	      setIsGenerationOver(true);
57	    }
58	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop]);
59	
60	  const handleDragOver = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, containerRef: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>) => {
61	    e.preventDefault();
62	    e.stopPropagation();
63	    
64	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
65	    
66	    if (dragType !== 'none' && containerRef.current) {
67	      const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
68	      // Account for timeline padding offset - same calculation as useTimelineDrag
69	      const relativeX = e.clientX - rect.left - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET;
70	      const effectiveWidth = rect.width - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
71	      const targetFrame = Math.max(0, pixelToFrame(relativeX, effectiveWidth, fullMin, fullRange));
72	      setDropTargetFrame(targetFrame);
73	      
74	      if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
75	        setIsFileOver(true);
76	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
77	      } else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
78	        setIsGenerationOver(true);
79	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy';
80	      } else {
81	        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
82	      }
83	    } else {
84	      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'none';
85	      setDropTargetFrame(null);
86	    }
87	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop, fullMin, fullRange]);
88	
89	  const handleDragLeave = useCallback((e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
90	    e.preventDefault();
91	    e.stopPropagation();
92	    
93	    // Only clear state if we're actually leaving the container
94	    if (e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node)) {
95	      return;
96	    }
97	    
98	    setIsFileOver(false);
99	    setIsGenerationOver(false);
100	    setDropTargetFrame(null);
101	  }, []);
102	
103	  const handleDrop = useCallback(async (e: React.DragEvent<HTMLDivElement>, containerRef?: React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement>) => {
104	    e.preventDefault();
105	    e.stopPropagation();
106	    
107	    const dragType = getDragType(e);
108	    
109	    // Calculate target frame directly from drop coordinates (not stale state)
110	    // This fixes the "jumping to wrong location" bug caused by stale dropTargetFrame state
111	    let targetFrame: number | null = dropTargetFrame;
112	    if (containerRef?.current) {
113	      const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
114	      const relativeX = e.clientX - rect.left - TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET;
115	      const effectiveWidth = rect.width - (TIMELINE_PADDING_OFFSET * 2);
116	      targetFrame = Math.max(0, pixelToFrame(relativeX, effectiveWidth, fullMin, fullRange));
117	    }
118	    
119	    // Reset visual state — always, even if a child variant handler already
120	    // processed the drop. This is the whole point: the event bubbles up so we
121	    // can clean up, but we skip re-processing the action.
122	    setIsFileOver(false);
123	    setIsGenerationOver(false);
124	    setDropTargetFrame(null);
125	
126	    // A child variant drop target already handled this drop — don't also
127	    // create a standalone image.
128	    if (wasDropHandledByVariant(e)) {
129	      return;
130	    }
131	
132	    // Handle file drops (from file system)
133	    if (dragType === 'file' && onFileDrop) {
134	      const files = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
135	      
136	      if (files.length === 0) {
137	        return;
138	      }
139	
140	      const validFiles = filterValidTimelineImageFiles(files);
141	
142	      if (validFiles.length === 0) {
143	        return;
144	      }
145	
146	      try {
147	        await onFileDrop(validFiles, targetFrame ?? undefined);
148	      } catch (error) {
149	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UnifiedDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
150	      }
151	    }
152	    
153	    // Handle generation drops (from GenerationsPane)
154	    else if (dragType === 'generation' && onGenerationDrop) {
155	      
156	      const data = getGenerationDropData(e);
157	      
158	      if (!data) {
159	        normalizeAndPresentError(new Error('No valid data found'), { context: 'UnifiedDrop', showToast: false });
160	        return;
161	      }
162	      
163	      try {
164	        
165	        await onGenerationDrop(data.generationId, data.imageUrl, data.thumbUrl, targetFrame ?? undefined);
166	      } catch (error) {
167	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'UnifiedDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add generation' });
168	      }
169	    }
170	  }, [getDragType, onFileDrop, onGenerationDrop, dropTargetFrame, fullMin, fullRange]);
171	
172	  // Determine current drag type for consumers
173	  const currentDragType: DragType = isFileOver ? 'file' : isGenerationOver ? 'generation' : 'none';
174	
175	  return {
176	    isFileOver: isFileOver || isGenerationOver, // Combined state for backward compatibility
177	    dropTargetFrame,
178	    dragType: currentDragType,
179	    handleDragEnter,
180	    handleDragOver,
181	    handleDragLeave,
182	    handleDrop,
183	  };
184	};
185

30	interface UseDropActionsProps {
31	  actions: Pick<ShotEditorActions, 'setUploadingImage' | 'setAutoAdjustedAspectRatio'>;
32	  selectedShot: Shot;
33	  projectId: string;
34	  batchVideoFrames: number;
35	}
36	
37	type ProjectWithSettings = {
38	  id: string;
39	  aspectRatio?: string;
40	  settings?: {
41	    aspectRatio?: string;
42	  };
43	};
44	
45	type AutoAdjustedAspectRatio = NonNullable<
46	  ShotEditorActions extends { setAutoAdjustedAspectRatio: (value: infer TValue) => void }
47	    ? TValue
48	    : never
49	>;
50	
51	async function readImageDimensions(file: File): Promise<{ width: number; height: number } | null> {
52	  try {
53	    return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
54	      const reader = new FileReader();
55	
56	      reader.onload = (event) => {
57	        const img = new Image();
58	
59	        img.onload = () => {
60	          resolve({ width: img.width, height: img.height });
61	        };
62	
63	        img.onerror = () => {
64	          reject(new Error('Failed to load dropped image.'));
65	        };
66	
67	        if (event.target?.result) {
68	          img.src = event.target.result as string;
69	          return;
70	        }
71	
72	        reject(new Error('Failed to read dropped image.'));
73	      };
74	
75	      reader.onerror = () => {
76	        reject(new Error('Failed to probe dropped image dimensions.'));
77	      };
78	
79	      reader.readAsDataURL(file);
80	    });
81	  } catch (error) {
82	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
83	      context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:probe',
84	      showToast: false,
85	    });
86	    return null;
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	async function maybeAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio(
91	  files: File[],
92	  currentShot: Shot,
93	  projectsList: ProjectWithSettings[],
94	  projectId: string,
95	): Promise<AutoAdjustedAspectRatio | null> {
96	  try {
97	    const firstFile = files[0];
98	    if (!firstFile) {
99	      return null;
100	    }
101	
102	    const dimensions = await readImageDimensions(firstFile);
103	    if (!dimensions?.width || !dimensions.height) {
104	      return null;
105	    }
106	
107	    const imageRatio = dimensions.width / dimensions.height;
108	    const closestAspectRatio = findClosestAspectRatio(imageRatio);
109	    const currentProject = projectsList.find((project) => project.id === projectId);
110	    const currentAspectRatio =
111	      currentShot.aspect_ratio ??
112	      currentProject?.aspectRatio ??
113	      currentProject?.settings?.aspectRatio ??
114	      '16:9';
115	
116	    if (closestAspectRatio === currentAspectRatio) {
117	      return null;
118	    }
119	
120	    return {
121	      previousAspectRatio: currentShot.aspect_ratio ?? null,
122	      adjustedTo: closestAspectRatio,
123	    };
124	  } catch (error) {
125	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
126	      context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio',
127	      showToast: false,
128	    });
129	    return null;
130	  }
131	}
132

306	      throw error;

339	        thumbUrl: thumbUrl,
340	        timelineFrame: targetFrame,
341	        project_id: currentProjectId
342	      });
343	
344	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that the new image is in place
345	      await demoteOrphanedVariantsRef.current(currentShot.id, 'image-add');
346	    } catch (error) {
347	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'GenerationDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add generation' });
348	      throw error;
349	    }
350	  }, []);
351	
352	  /**
353	   * Handle dropping external images onto batch mode grid
354	   */
355	  const handleBatchImageDrop = useCallback(async (
356	    files: File[],
357	    targetFrame?: number
358	  ) => {
359	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
360	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
361	    let adjustedShot: Shot | undefined;
362	
363	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {
364	      toast.error("Cannot add images: No shot or project selected.");
365	      return;
366	    }
367	
368	    // Track optimistic items for cleanup
369	    const optimisticIds: string[] = [];
370	    const localUrls: string[] = [];
371	
372	    try {
373	      try {
374	        const autoAdjustedAspectRatio = await maybeAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio(
375	          files,
376	          currentShot,
377	          projectsRef.current as ProjectWithSettings[],
378	          currentProjectId
379	        );
380	
381	        if (autoAdjustedAspectRatio) {
382	          const ok = await updateShotAspectRatioRef.current(
383	            currentShot.id,
384	            currentProjectId,
385	            autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
386	            { immediate: true }
387	          );
388	
389	          if (ok) {
390	            setAutoAdjustedAspectRatioRef.current(autoAdjustedAspectRatio);
391	            adjustedShot = {
392	              ...currentShot,
393	              aspect_ratio: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
394	            };
395	          } else {
396	            normalizeAndPresentError(
397	              new Error('Failed to auto-adjust shot aspect ratio for the dropped image.'),
398	              {
399	                context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
400	                showToast: false,
401	                logData: {
402	                  shotId: currentShot.id,
403	                  adjustedTo: autoAdjustedAspectRatio.adjustedTo,
404	                },
405	              }
406	            );
407	          }
408	        }
409	      } catch (error) {
410	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, {
411	          context: 'DropAutoAdjustShotAspectRatio:update',
412	          showToast: false,
413	          logData: { shotId: currentShot.id },
414	        });
415	      }
416	
417	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(true);
418	
419	      // 1. Calculate target frame positions with collision detection
420	      const startFrame = targetFrame ?? await fetchNextAvailableFrameForShot(currentShot.id, undefined);
421	
422	      // For multiple files, ensure each position is unique
423	      const existingGens = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
424	      const existingFrames = existingGens
425	        .filter(g => g.timeline_frame != null && g.timeline_frame !== -1)
426	        .map(g => g.timeline_frame as number);
427	
428	      const positions: number[] = [];
429	      const allUsedFrames = [...existingFrames];
430	      for (let i = 0; i < files.length; i++) {
431	        let targetFrame = startFrame + (i * DEFAULT_FRAME_SPACING);
432	        while (allUsedFrames.includes(targetFrame)) {
433	          targetFrame += 1;
434	        }
435	        positions.push(targetFrame);
436	        allUsedFrames.push(targetFrame);
437	      }
438	
439	      // 2. Create optimistic entries immediately using local file URLs
440	      const previousFastGens = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
441	
442	      const optimisticItems = files.map((file, index) => {
443	        const localUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file);
444	        localUrls.push(localUrl);
445	        const tempId = `temp-upload-${Date.now()}-${index}-${Math.random()}`;
446	        optimisticIds.push(tempId);
447	
448	        return {
449	          id: tempId,
450	          generation_id: tempId,
451	          shotImageEntryId: tempId,
452	          shot_generation_id: tempId,
453	          location: localUrl,
454	          thumbnail_url: localUrl,
455	          imageUrl: localUrl,
456	          thumbUrl: localUrl,
457	          timeline_frame: positions[index],
458	          type: 'image' as const,
459	          created_at: new Date().toISOString(),
460	          starred: false,
461	          name: file.name,
462	          based_on: null,
463	          params: {},
464	          shot_data: { [currentShot.id]: [positions[index]] },
465	          _optimistic: true,
466	          _uploading: true
467	        };
468	      });
469	
470	      // Add optimistic items to cache
471	      queryClientRef.current.setQueryData(
472	        generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id),
473	        [...previousFastGens, ...optimisticItems]
474	      );
475	
476	      // 3. Crop images
477	      const processedFiles = await cropImagesToShotAspectRatio(
478	        files,
479	        adjustedShot ?? currentShot,
480	        currentProjectId,
481	        projectsRef.current,
482	        uploadSettingsRef.current
483	      );
484	
485	      // 4. Upload with positions
486	      const result = await handleExternalImageDropMutationRef.current.mutateAsync({
487	        imageFiles: processedFiles,
488	        targetShotId: currentShot.id,
489	        [REDACTED],
490	        currentShotCount: 0,
491	        skipAutoPosition: false,
492	        positions: positions,
493	      });
494	
495	      if (!result?.generationIds?.length) {
496	        return;
497	      }
498	
499	      // 5. If positions weren't set by the upload, set them now (fallback)
500	      const { data: checkData } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
501	        .select('id, timeline_frame')
502	        .eq('shot_id', currentShot.id)
503	        .in('generation_id', result.generationIds)
504	        .limit(1);
505	
506	      if (checkData?.[0]?.timeline_frame === null) {
507	        await persistTimelinePositions(
508	          currentShot.id,
509	          result.generationIds,
510	          startFrame,
511	          1 // Use 1 frame spacing for batch mode
512	        );
513	      }
514	
515	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that new images are in place
516	      await demoteOrphanedVariantsRef.current(currentShot.id, 'image-add');
517	
518	    } catch (error) {
519	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'BatchDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add images' });
520	
521	      // Remove optimistic items on error
522	      const currentCache = queryClientRef.current.getQueryData<GenerationRow[]>(generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id)) || [];
523	      queryClientRef.current.setQueryData(
524	        generationQueryKeys.byShot(currentShot.id),
525	        currentCache.filter(item => !optimisticIds.includes(item.id))
526	      );
527	
528	      throw error;
529	    } finally {
530	      actionsRef.current.setUploadingImage(false);
531	
532	      // Clean up local URLs to prevent memory leaks
533	      localUrls.forEach(url => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
534	    }
535	  }, []);
536	
537	  /**
538	   * Handle dropping a generation from GenerationsPane onto batch mode grid
539	   */
540	  const handleBatchGenerationDrop = useCallback(async (
541	    generationId: string,
542	    imageUrl: string,
543	    thumbUrl: string | undefined,
544	    targetFrame?: number
545	  ) => {
546	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
547	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
548	
549	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {
550	      toast.error("Cannot add generation: No shot or project selected.");
551	      return;
552	    }
553	
554	    if (!generationId) {
555	      toast.error("Invalid generation: Missing generation ID.");
556	      return;
557	    }
558	
559	    try {
560	      await addImageToShotMutationRef.current.mutateAsync({
561	        generation_id: generationId,
562	        shot_id: currentShot.id,
563	        imageUrl: imageUrl,
564	        thumbUrl: thumbUrl,
565	        project_id: currentProjectId,
566	        timelineFrame: targetFrame,
567	      });
568	
569	      // Demote orphaned video variants now that the new image is in place
570	      await demoteOrphanedVariantsRef.current(currentShot.id, 'image-add');
571	    } catch (error) {
572	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'BatchDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add generation' });
573	      throw error;
574	    }
575	  }, []);
576	
577	  const handleVariantDrop = useCallback(async (params: VariantDropParams) => {
578	    const currentShot = selectedShotRef.current;
579	    const currentProjectId = projectIdRef.current;
580	
581	    if (!currentShot?.id || !currentProjectId) {
582	      toast.error("Cannot add variant: No shot or project selected.");
583	      return;
584	    }
585	
586	    try {
587	      if (params.sourceGenerationId) {
588	        if (!params.imageUrl) {
589	          toast.error("Cannot add variant: Missing source image.");
590	          return;
591	        }
592	
593	        const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants').insert({
594	          generation_id: params.targetGenerationId,
595	          project_id: currentProjectId,
596	          location: params.imageUrl,
597	          thumbnail_url: params.thumbUrl || params.imageUrl,
598	          is_primary: params.mode === 'main',
599	          variant_type: VARIANT_TYPE.DROPPED,
600	          params: {
601	            source: 'generation-drop',
602	            source_generation_id: params.sourceGenerationId,
603	            source_variant_id: params.sourceVariantId || null,
604	          },
605	        });
606	
607	        if (error) {
608	          throw error;
609	        }
610	      } else if (params.files?.length) {
611	        const [file] = params.files;
612	        if (!file) {
613	          return;
614	        }
615	
616	        const { imageUrl, thumbnailUrl } = await uploadImageForVariant(file, currentProjectId);
617	        const { error } = await supabase().from('generation_variants').insert({
618	          generation_id: params.targetGenerationId,
619	          project_id: currentProjectId,
620	          location: imageUrl,
621	          thumbnail_url: thumbnailUrl || imageUrl,
622	          is_primary: params.mode === 'main',
623	          variant_type: VARIANT_TYPE.DROPPED,
624	          params: {
625	            source: 'file-drop',
626	            original_filename: file.name,
627	          },
628	        });
629	
630	        if (error) {
631	          throw error;
632	        }
633	      } else {
634	        return;
635	      }
636	
637	      await enqueueVariantInvalidation(queryClientRef.current, {
638	        generationId: params.targetGenerationId,
639	        shotId: currentShot.id,
640	        reason: 'variant-drop',
641	      });
642	
643	      if (params.mode === 'main') {
644	        await queryClientRef.current.invalidateQueries({
645	          [REDACTED](currentShot.id),
646	        });
647	      }
648	    } catch (error) {
649	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'VariantDrop', toastTitle: 'Failed to add variant' });
650	      throw error;
651	    }
652	  }, []);
653	
654	  return {
655	    handleTimelineImageDrop,
656	    handleTimelineGenerationDrop,
657	    handleBatchImageDrop,
658	    handleBatchGenerationDrop,
659	    handleVariantDrop,
660	  };
661	};
662

16	export const useCreateShotWithGenerations = () => {
17	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
18	
19	  return useMutation({
20	    mutationFn: async ({
21	      projectId,
22	      shotName,
23	      generationIds,
24	    }: {
25	      projectId: string;
26	      shotName: string;
27	      generationIds: string[];
28	    }) => {
29	      const { data, error } = await supabase().rpc('create_shot_with_generations', {
30	          p_project_id: projectId,
31	          p_shot_name: shotName,
32	          p_generation_ids: generationIds,
33	        })
34	        .single();
35	
36	      if (error) {
37	        throw error;
38	      }
39	
40	      const typedData = data as CreateShotWithGenerationsRpcResult | null;
41	      if (!typedData?.success) {
42	        throw new Error('Failed to create shot with generations');
43	      }
44	
45	      return typedData;
46	    },
47	
48	    onSuccess: (_data, variables) => {
49	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
50	        queryKey: [...queryKeys.shots.all, variables.projectId],
51	        refetchType: 'inactive',
52	      });
53	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({
54	        [REDACTED](variables.projectId),
55	        refetchType: 'inactive',
56	      });
57	    },
58	
59	    onError: (error: Error) => {
60	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation', toastTitle: 'Failed to create shot with generations' });
61	    },
62	  });
63	};

65	export const useHandleExternalImageDrop = () => {
66	  const createShotMutation = useCreateShot();
67	  const addImageToShotMutation = useAddImageToShot();
68	
69	  return useMutation({
70	    mutationFn: async (variables: ExternalImageDropVariables) => {
71	      const { currentProjectQueryKey } = variables;
72	      if (!currentProjectQueryKey) {
73	        toast.error('Cannot add image(s): current project is not identified.');
74	        return null;
75	      }
76	
77	      try {
78	        return await processDroppedImages({
79	          variables,

82	          addImageToShot: addImageToShotMutation.mutateAsync,
83	          addImageToShotWithoutPosition: addImageToShotMutation.mutateAsyncWithoutPosition,
84	        });
85	      } catch (error) {
86	        normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'useShotCreation', toastTitle: 'Failed to process dropped image(s)' });
87	        return null;
88	      }
89	    },
90	  });
91	};

180	export async function createGenerationForLocalFile(

185	    mediaType: input.mediaType,

188	  await saveHandle(localHandleId, input.handle);

193	    mediaType: input.mediaType,
194	    thumbnailUrl,
195	    localHandleId,
196	  });
197	}

218	    thumbnailUrl: thumbnailUrl || imageUrl,
219	    generationParams,
220	  });
221	}

50	 * Crop images to match the shot's aspect ratio
51	 * @returns Processed files (cropped or original if cropping fails/disabled)
52	 */
53	export const cropImagesToShotAspectRatio = async (
54	  files: File[],
55	  selectedShot: Shot | undefined,
56	  projectId: string,
57	  projects: Project[],
58	  uploadSettings: UploadSettings | undefined
59	): Promise<File[]> => {
60	  // Skip cropping if disabled
61	  if (uploadSettings?.cropToProjectSize === false) {
62	    return files;
63	  }
64	
65	  // Determine aspect ratio (prioritize shot over project)
66	  const currentProject = projects.find(p => p.id === projectId);
67	  const aspectRatioStr = selectedShot?.aspect_ratio || 
68	                        currentProject?.aspectRatio || 
69	                        currentProject?.settings?.aspectRatio;
70	  
71	  if (!aspectRatioStr) {
72	    return files;
73	  }
74	
75	  const targetAspectRatio = parseRatio(aspectRatioStr);
76	  
77	  if (isNaN(targetAspectRatio)) {
78	    return files;
79	  }
80	
81	  // Crop each file
82	  const cropPromises = files.map(async (file) => {
83	    try {
84	      const result = await cropImageToProjectAspectRatio(file, targetAspectRatio);
85	      if (result) {
86	        return result.croppedFile;
87	      }
88	      return file; // Return original if cropping fails
89	    } catch (error) {
90	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'ImageCrop', showToast: false });
91	      return file; // Return original on error
92	    }
93	  });
94	
95	  return await Promise.all(cropPromises);
96	};

103	
104	export const fetchNextAvailableFrameForShot = async (
105	  shotId: string,
106	  targetFrame: number | undefined
107	): Promise<number> => {
108	  
109	  // Query shot_generations directly from database to get current positions
110	  const { data: shotGenerationsData, error } = await supabase().from('shot_generations')
111	    .select(`
112	      id,
113	      generation_id,
114	      timeline_frame,
115	      generation:generations!shot_generations_generation_id_generations_id_fk (
116	        id,
117	        location,
118	        type
119	      )
120	    `)
121	    .eq('shot_id', shotId)
122	    .order('timeline_frame', { ascending: true });
123	
124	  if (error) {
125	    normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'AddImagesDebug', showToast: false });
126	    // Default to 0 if query fails
127	    return targetFrame !== undefined ? targetFrame : 0;
128	  }
129	
130	  if (!shotGenerationsData) {
131	    return targetFrame !== undefined ? targetFrame : 0;
132	  }
133	
134	  // Filter out videos using canonical function from typeGuards
135	  const filteredShotGenerations = shotGenerationsData.filter(shotGen =>
136	    shotGen.generation && !isVideoShotGenerations(shotGen as ShotGenerationsLike)
137	  );
138	
139	  // Get positions only from items with valid timeline_frame
140	  const existingPositions = filteredShotGenerations
141	    .filter(shotGen => shotGen.timeline_frame !== null && shotGen.timeline_frame !== undefined && shotGen.timeline_frame !== -1)
142	    .map(shotGen => shotGen.timeline_frame!);
143	  
144	  // If target frame provided, ensure it's unique
145	  if (targetFrame !== undefined) {
146	    const uniqueFrame = ensureUniqueFrame(targetFrame, existingPositions);
147	    return uniqueFrame;
148	  }
149

100	export async function runImageDropInterceptor({
101	  files,
102	  targetFrame,
103	  onFileDrop,
104	  setPendingDropFrame,
105	}: ImageDropInterceptorArgs): Promise<void> {
106	  if (targetFrame !== undefined) {
107	    setPendingDropFrame(targetFrame);
108	  }
109	  if (onFileDrop) {
110	    await onFileDrop(files, targetFrame);
111	  }
112	}
113	
114	export async function runGenerationDropInterceptor({
115	  generationId,
116	  imageUrl,
117	  thumbUrl,
118	  targetFrame,
119	  onGenerationDrop,
120	  setPendingDropFrame,
121	  setIsInternalDropProcessing,
122	}: GenerationDropInterceptorArgs): Promise<void> {
123	  if (targetFrame !== undefined) {
124	    setPendingDropFrame(targetFrame);
125	    setIsInternalDropProcessing(true);
126	  }
127	  try {
128	    if (onGenerationDrop) {
129	      await onGenerationDrop(generationId, imageUrl, thumbUrl, targetFrame);
130	    }

The plan gathers handles from all e.dataTransfer.items where kind === 'file' and pairs them with files by index, but after filtering the validFiles array can be shorter than the items-derived handles array. The plan only says 'if the count matches, resolve the handle promises' without specifying behavior when counts diverge, so a single non-image file in the drop will silently misalign every subsequent handle with the wrong File and the gate may attach a handle whose bytes do not match the stored file.\",\n      \"category\": \"correctness\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-significant\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/imageDropValidation.ts:5-13 (filter by MIME); src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useUnifiedDrop.ts:134-147 (Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files) then filtered).\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-003\",\n      \"concern\": \"First crop step happens before the mutation, so the plan's reference-equality crop guard is incomplete. useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop calls cropImagesToShotAspectRatio at line 252 BEFORE passing files to handleExternalImageDropMutation, then processDroppedImages calls cropFilesIfNeeded again (externalImageDrop.ts:325). The plan only checks processedFiles[i] === originalFiles[i] inside processSingleDroppedImage against the second crop's input, but by then the first crop in useDropActions may have already produced new File references that do not match the captured handle's bytes. The handle was for the originally-dropped file. If cropImagesToShotAspectRatio modifies any file, the handle should be cleared at that boundary too — otherwise the local-handle path stores a generation whose persisted handle returns different bytes than what is on the timeline.\",\n      \"category\": \"correctness\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-significant\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useDropActions.ts:252-273 (cropImagesToShotAspectRatio before mutation); src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/editor-state/timelineDropHelpers.ts:53-96 (returns new File when cropped); src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:325 (cropFilesIfNeeded called again).\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-004\",\n      \"concern\": \"Shared FileDropHandler type widening unaddressed. ShotSettingsContext.types.ts:49 declares onFileDrop: FileDropHandler, where FileDropHandler is the shared type at src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts:15-18 used by non-timeline consumers (modal, batch, gallery). The plan adds a third optional handles parameter on onFileDrop but does not say whether to widen this shared type or introduce a separate timeline-specific signature. If the shared type is widened, the plan's named-files list is incomplete; if it is not widened, the new handles parameter cannot type-check through the FileDropHandler-typed slot in ShotSettingsContext without a cast.\",\n      \"category\": \"maintainability\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts:15-18; src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/ShotSettingsContext.types.ts:49.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-005\",\n      \"concern\": \"Verification plan lacks an explicit unit test for the interceptor layer. The plan says 'useUnifiedDrop.test.ts (if present)' but does not require a test for runImageDropInterceptor / handleImageDropInterceptor in useTimelineOrchestratorActions, which is the layer most likely to silently drop the new handles parameter (and is the layer the original brief specifically called out as 'strips/discards DataTransferItem info'). Without a regression test at that boundary, a future refactor can re-strip handles and fall back to the upload path without any test failing.\",\n      \"category\": \"verifiability\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/timeline-core/useTimelineOrchestratorActions.ts:100-112 and :229-236 (interceptor functions); plan Step 6 names only externalImageDrop.test.ts and useDropActions.test.ts.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"verified_flag_ids\": [],\n  \"disputed_flag_ids\": []\n}\n"

The plan gathers handles from all e.dataTransfer.items where kind === 'file' and pairs them with files by index, but after filtering, validFiles can be shorter than the items-derived handles array. The plan only says 'if the count matches, resolve the handle promises' without specifying behavior when counts diverge, so a single non-image file in the drop will silently misalign every subsequent handle with the wrong File and the gate may persist a handle whose bytes do not match the generation row.\",\n      \"category\": \"correctness\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-significant\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/imageDropValidation.ts:5-13 (MIME filter); src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/drag/useUnifiedDrop.ts:134-147.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-003\",\n      \"concern\": \"First crop happens before the mutation, so the plan's reference-equality crop guard is incomplete. useDropActions.handleTimelineImageDrop calls cropImagesToShotAspectRatio at line 252 BEFORE passing files to handleExternalImageDropMutation, then processDroppedImages calls cropFilesIfNeeded again (externalImageDrop.ts:325). The plan only checks processedFiles[i] === originalFiles[i] inside processSingleDroppedImage against the second crop's input, but by then the first crop in useDropActions may have already produced new File references whose bytes do not match the captured handle. The handle is for the originally-dropped file, so if cropImagesToShotAspectRatio modifies any file, the handle must be cleared at that boundary too -- otherwise the local-handle path persists a generation whose handle returns different bytes than what is on the timeline.\",\n      \"category\": \"correctness\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-significant\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/actions/useDropActions.ts:252-273; src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/hooks/editor-state/timelineDropHelpers.ts:53-96 (returns new File when cropped); src/shared/hooks/shots/externalImageDrop.ts:325.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-004\",\n      \"concern\": \"Shared FileDropHandler type widening unaddressed. ShotSettingsContext.types.ts:49 declares onFileDrop: FileDropHandler, where FileDropHandler is the shared type at src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts:15-18 used by non-timeline consumers (modal, batch, gallery). The plan adds a third optional handles parameter on onFileDrop but does not say whether to widen this shared type or introduce a separate timeline-specific signature. If the shared type is widened, the named-files list is incomplete; if it is not widened, the new handles parameter cannot type-check through the FileDropHandler-typed slot without a cast.\",\n      \"category\": \"maintainability\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/shared/types/imageHandlers.ts:15-18; src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/ShotSettingsContext.types.ts:49.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"FLAG-005\",\n      \"concern\": \"Verification plan lacks an explicit unit test for the interceptor layer. The plan says 'useUnifiedDrop.test.ts (if present)' but does not require a test for runImageDropInterceptor or handleImageDropInterceptor in useTimelineOrchestratorActions -- the layer the brief specifically called out as where DataTransferItem info gets stripped. Without a regression test at that boundary, a future refactor can silently re-strip handles and the timeline will fall back to the upload path with no test failing.\",\n      \"category\": \"verifiability\",\n      \"severity_hint\": \"likely-minor\",\n      \"evidence\": \"src/tools/travel-between-images/components/Timeline/hooks/timeline-core/useTimelineOrchestratorActions.ts:100-112,229-236; plan Step 6 names only externalImageDrop.test.ts and useDropActions.test.ts.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"verified_flag_ids\": [],\n  \"disputed_flag_ids\": []\n}\n"
}
